class="text-[1.75em] font-semibold">Using RevenueCat</h1><p class="mb-8 text-lg text-fd-muted-foreground">If you want 
to use RevenueCat to handle your subscription-related logic with Superwall, follow this guide.</p><div class="flex 
flex-row gap-2 items-center border-b -mt-4 pb-6"><button class="inline-flex items-center justify-center rounded-md p-2

min-w-0 w-full text-sky-200"><p>Not using RevenueCat? No problem! Superwall works out of the box without any 
additional SDKs.</p></span></div>
  <div class="flex items-start gap-4 rounded-[var(--radius-lg)] border border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-500/10 py-3 px-4 
my-4"><span class="mt-0.5 w-4"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 24 24" 
fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide 
lucide-triangle-alert w-4 h-4 text-amber-300/80" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m21.73 18-8-14a2 2 0 0 0-3.48 0l-8 14A2 2 
0 0 0 4 21h16a2 2 0 0 0 1.73-3"></path><path d="M12 9v4"></path><path d="M12 17h.01"></path></svg></span><span 
class="text-sm prose min-w-0 w-full text-amber-200">You only need to use a <code>PurchaseController</code> if you want 
end-to-end control of the purchasing pipeline. The recommended way to use RevenueCat with Superwall is by putting it 
in observer mode.</span></div>
  <p>You can integrate RevenueCat with Superwall in one of two ways:</p>
  <ul>
  <li><a href="#custompurchasecontrollerprovider-component"><code>CustomPurchaseControllerProvider</code> component 
(recommended)</a></li>
  <li><a href="#purchasecontroller-legacy"><code>PurchaseController</code> (legacy)</a></li>
  </ul>
  <h2 class="flex scroll-m-28 flex-row items-center gap-2" id="custompurchasecontrollerprovider-component"><a 
data-card="" href="#custompurchasecontrollerprovider-component" 
class="peer"><code>CustomPurchaseControllerProvider</code> component</a><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" 
width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" 
stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-link size-3.5 shrink-0 text-fd-muted-foreground opacity-0 
transition-opacity peer-hover:opacity-100" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M10 13a5 5 0 0 0 7.54.54l3-3a5 5 0 0 
0-7.07-7.07l-1.72 1.71"></path><path d="M14 11a5 5 0 0 0-7.54-.54l-3 3a5 5 0 0 0 7.07 
7.07l1.71-1.71"></path></svg></h2>
  <p>The easiest way to integrate RevenueCat with Superwall is using the <code>CustomPurchaseControllerProvider</code> 
component. This approach uses modern React patterns and requires much less code.</p>
  <h3 class="flex scroll-m-28 flex-row items-center gap-2" id="1-configure-revenuecat-and-superwall"><a data-card="" 
href="#1-configure-revenuecat-and-superwall" class="peer">1. Configure RevenueCat and Superwall</a><svg

7.07l1.71-1.71"></path></svg></h3>

  <p>On Android, <code>onPurchase</code> includes a <code>basePlanId</code> and may include an <code>offerId</code>. 
Use those values to find the matching RevenueCat subscription option and call 
<code>Purchases.purchaseSubscriptionOption(option)</code>. Calling 
<code>Purchases.purchaseStoreProduct(product)</code> for a Google Play subscription lets RevenueCat choose the 
product&#x27;s default offer, which may not be the offer selected on the Superwall paywall.</p>
  <h3 class="flex scroll-m-28 flex-row items-center gap-2" id="2-sync-subscription-status"><a data-card=""

  <p>That&#x27;s it! This approach is much simpler than the class-based implementation and uses modern React 
patterns.</p>
  <p>Check out our sample app for a working example: <a 
href="https://github.com/superwall/expo-superwall/tree/main/example" rel="noreferrer noopener" target="_blank">Expo 
Example</a></p>
  <hr/>

  <div class="flex items-start gap-4 rounded-[var(--radius-lg)] border border-sky-500/30 bg-sky-500/10 py-3 px-4

x2="12" y1="8" y2="12"></line><line x1="12" x2="12.01" y1="16" y2="16"></line></svg></span><span class="text-sm prose 
min-w-0 w-full text-sky-200"><p>This approach is for apps using the legacy <code>expo-superwall/compat</code> import. 
For new projects, use the hooks-based integration above.</p></span></div>
  <p>You can integrate RevenueCat with Superwall using purchase controllers:</p>
  <ol>
  <li><strong>Using a purchase controller:</strong> Use this route if you want to maintain control over purchasing 
logic and code.</li>
  <li><strong>Using PurchasesAreCompletedBy:</strong> Here, you don&#x27;t use a purchase controller and you tell 
RevenueCat that purchases are completed by your app using StoreKit. In this mode, RevenueCat will observe the 
purchases that the Superwall SDK makes. For more info <a 
href="https://www.revenuecat.com/docs/migrating-to-revenuecat/sdk-or-not/finishing-transactions" rel="noreferrer 
noopener" target="_blank">see here</a>.</li>
  </ol>
  <h3 class="flex scroll-m-28 flex-row items-center gap-2" id="1-create-a-purchasecontroller"><a data-card="" 
href="#1-create-a-purchasecontroller" class="peer">1. Create a PurchaseController</a><svg

7.07l1.71-1.71"></path></svg></h3>

  <p>As discussed in <a href="/docs/expo/guides/advanced-configuration">Purchases and Subscription Status</a>, this 
<code>PurchaseController</code> is responsible for handling the subscription-related logic. Take a few moments to look 
through the code to understand how it does this.</p>
  <h3 class="flex scroll-m-28 flex-row items-center gap-2" id="2-configure-superwall"><a data-card="" 
href="#2-configure-superwall" class="peer">2. Configure Superwall</a><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg"

7.07l1.71-1.71"></path></svg></h3>

<code>Superwall.configure(apiKey:purchaseController)</code>:</p>

  <h3 class="flex scroll-m-28 flex-row items-center gap-2" id="3-sync-the-subscription-status"><a data-card="" 
href="#3-sync-the-subscription-status" class="peer">3. Sync the subscription status</a><svg 
xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" 
stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-link size-3.5 shrink-0 
text-fd-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity peer-hover:opacity-100" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M10 13a5 5 0 
0 0 7.54.54l3-3a5 5 0 0 0-7.07-7.07l-1.72 1.71"></path><path d="M14 11a5 5 0 0 0-7.54-.54l-3 3a5 5 0 0 0 7.07 
7.07l1.71-1.71"></path></svg></h3>
  <p>Then, call <code>purchaseController.syncSubscriptionStatus()</code> to keep Superwall&#x27;s subscription status 
up to date with RevenueCat.</p>
  <p>That&#x27;s it! Check out our sample app for working examples:</p>
  <ul>
  <li><a href="https://github.com/superwall/expo-superwall/tree/main/example" rel="noreferrer noopener" 
target="_blank">Expo</a></li>
  <li><a href="https://github.com/superwall/react-native-superwall/blob/main/example/src/RCPurchaseController.tsx" 
rel="noreferrer noopener" target="_blank">React Native (deprecated)</a></li>
  </ul>
  <h3 class="flex scroll-m-28 flex-row items-center gap-2" id="using-purchasesarecompletedby"><a data-card="" 
href="#using-purchasesarecompletedby" class="peer">Using PurchasesAreCompletedBy</a><svg 
xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="24" height="24" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" 
stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" class="lucide lucide-link size-3.5 shrink-0 
text-fd-muted-foreground opacity-0 transition-opacity peer-hover:opacity-100" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M10 13a5 5 0 
0 0 7.54.54l3-3a5 5 0 0 0-7.07-7.07l-1.72 1.71"></path><path d="M14 11a5 5 0 0 0-7.54-.54l-3 3a5 5 0 0 0 7.07 
7.07l1.71-1.71"></path></svg></h3>
  <p>If you&#x27;re using RevenueCat&#x27;s <a 
href="https://www.revenuecat.com/docs/migrating-to-revenuecat/sdk-or-not/finishing-transactions" rel="noreferrer 
noopener" target="_blank">PurchasesAreCompletedBy</a>, you don&#x27;t need to create a purchase controller. Register 
your placements, present a paywall â€” and Superwall will take care of completing any purchase the user starts. 
However, there are a few things to note if you use this setup:</p>
  <ol>
  <li>Here, you aren&#x27;t using RevenueCat&#x27;s <a 
href="https://www.revenuecat.com/docs/getting-started/entitlements#entitlements" rel="noreferrer noopener" 
target="_blank">entitlements</a> as a source of truth. If your app is multiplatform, you&#x27;ll need to consider how 
to link up pro features or purchased products for users.</li>
  <li>If you require custom logic when purchases occur, then you&#x27;ll want to add a purchase controller. In that 
case, Superwall handles purchasing flows and RevenueCat will still observe transactions to power their analytics and 
charts.</li>
  <li>Be sure that user identifiers are set the same way across Superwall and RevenueCat.</li>
  </ol>
  <p>For more information on observer mode, visit <a 
href="https://www.revenuecat.com/docs/migrating-to-revenuecat/sdk-or-not/finishing-transactions" rel="noreferrer 
noopener" target="_blank">RevenueCat&#x27;s docs</a>.</p></div><div data-state="closed" class="border-y py-3"><div 
class="flex flex-row items-center gap-2"><p class="text-sm font-medium pe-2">How is this guide?</p><button

ons/facebook-pixel",$ref:$R[381]={file:"integrations/facebook-pixel.mdx"}},$R[382]={$id:"integrations/adjust.mdx",type:
"page",name:"Adjust",description:"The Adjust integration automatically sends Superwall subscription and payment events 
to Adjust via the server-to-server (S2S) event API. Configure per-platform app tokens, map individual event tokens, 
and track subscription lifecycle events with device-level attribution.",url:"/docs/integrations/adjust",$ref:$R[383]={f
ile:"integrations/adjust.mdx"}},$R[384]={$id:"integrations/mixpanel.mdx",type:"page",name:"Mixpanel",description:"The

tude.mdx",type:"page",name:"Amplitude",description:"The Amplitude integration automatically sends Superwall

tude",$ref:$R[387]={file:"integrations/amplitude.mdx"}},$R[388]={$id:"integrations/customer-io.mdx",type:"page",name:"C
ustomer.io",description:"The Customer.io integration sends subscription lifecycle events from Superwall to

on subscription behavior, and track the complete customer journey from trial to paid subscriber.",url:"/docs/integratio
ns/customer-io",$ref:$R[389]={file:"integrations/customer-io.mdx"}},$R[390]={$id:"integrations/firebase.mdx",type:"page
",name:"Firebase",description:"The Firebase integration automatically sends Superwall subscription and payment events 
to Firebase Analytics (Google Analytics 4) using the Measurement Protocol. Track subscription lifecycle events, 
analyze revenue metrics, and leverage Firebase's powerful analytics capabilities.",url:"/docs/integrations/firebase",$r
ef:$R[391]={file:"integrations/firebase.mdx"}},$R[392]={$id:"integrations/statsig.mdx",type:"page",name:"Statsig",descr
iption:"The Statsig integration allows you to automatically send Superwall subscription and payment events to your

alytics.",url:"/docs/integrations/statsig",$ref:$R[393]={file:"integrations/statsig.mdx"}},$R[394]={$id:"integrations/a
ppstack.mdx",type:"page",name:"Appstack",description:"The Appstack integration forwards Superwall webhook events 
directly to Appstack for analytics and attribution. As a pass-through integration, it sends the raw event payload 
without transformation, giving Appstack full access to your subscription lifecycle data.",url:"/docs/integrations/appst
ack",$ref:$R[395]={file:"integrations/appstack.mdx"}},$R[396]={$id:"integrations/appstance.mdx",type:"page",name:"AppSt
ance",description:"The AppStance integration sends Superwall subscription and payment events to the AppStance platform

campaign performance analysis.",url:"/docs/integrations/appstance",$ref:$R[397]={file:"integrations/appstance.mdx"}},$R
[398]={$id:"integrations/slack.mdx",type:"page",name:"Slack",description:"The Slack integration sends real-time

cancellations, renewals, and revenue changes with rich, color-coded messages and contextual emojis.",url:"/docs/integra
tions/slack",$ref:$R[399]={file:"integrations/slack.mdx"}},$R[400]={$id:"integrations/discord.mdx",type:"page",name:"Di

ons/discord",$ref:$R[401]={file:"integrations/discord.mdx"}},$R[402]={$id:"integrations/figma-plugin.mdx",type:"page",n
